All `bin/` scripts are bash with a usage header, but this one is JS and Node 22 is already a hard dependency — so a node script with the same header style. Generalizing it beyond the hardcoded paths.

**→ Bash**

````json
{
  "command": "cat > bin/transcript <<'SCRIPT'\n#!/usr/bin/env node\n//\n// Renders a Claude Code session as a Markdown transcript.\n//\n//   bin/transcript                    the most recent session for this repo\n//   bin/transcript <session-id>       one named session\n//   bin/transcript <file.jsonl>       a log anywhere on disk\n//   bin/transcript <src> <dest.md>    an explicit destination\n//\n// Writes planning/TRANSCRIPT-<the day it ended>.md unless told otherwise.\n//\n// Claude Code keeps each session as JSONL under ~/.claude/projects/<the repo\n// path with every non-alphanumeric character turned into a dash>. That format\n// exists to be replayed into a model rather than read by a person, and there is\n// no export command in the CLI — hence this.\n//\n// Two things about the source are worth knowing before trusting any rendering\n// of it, including this one:\n//\n//   - **The reasoning is not in it.** Every `thinking` block is an empty string\n//     carrying a signature and nothing else. It is not persisted and cannot be\n//     recovered, so a transcript is what was said and done, never what was\n//     weighed. The blocks are dropped rather than rendered as empty boxes.\n//   - **Not every user-role record is the user.** The harness injects context as\n//     `<system-reminder>` blocks in user-role messages. Counting those as\n//     prompts is the obvious mistake and it inflates the turn count — this one\n//     session had 13 real prompts and 19 user-role records. They are kept, and\n//     labelled as what they are.\n//\n// The only change made to any body text is that headings inside replies are\n// demoted by two levels, so they cannot collide with the turn headings and\n// leave the document with no usable outline.\n\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst os = require('os');\nconst path = require('path');\n\nconst repoRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');\nconst logDir = path.join(\n  os.homedir(),\n  '.claude',\n  'projects',\n  repoRoot.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '-')\n);\n\nfunction resolveSource(arg) {\n  if (arg && fs.existsSync(arg)) return arg;\n  if (arg) {\n    const named = path.join(logDir, `${arg}.jsonl`);\n    if (fs.existsSync(named)) return named;\n    console.error(`No session log for \"${arg}\" — looked in ${logDir}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  if (!fs.existsSync(logDir)) {\n    console.error(`No session logs for this repo (${logDir} does not exist)`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  const newest = fs\n    .readdirSync(logDir)\n    .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.jsonl'))\n    .map((f) => path.join(logDir, f))\n    .map((f) => ({ f, at: fs.statSync(f).mtimeMs }))\n    .sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at)[0];\n  if (!newest) {\n    console.error(`No .jsonl session logs in ${logDir}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  return newest.f;\n}\n\nconst src = resolveSource(process.argv[2]);\nconst records = fs\n  .readFileSync(src, 'utf8')\n  .trim()\n  .split('\\n')\n  .map((l) => {\n    try {\n      return JSON.parse(l);\n    } catch {\n      return null;\n    }\n  })\n  .filter(Boolean);\n\nconst esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/\\r/g, '');\n\n// A body containing a fence needs a longer one, or it closes early.\nconst fence = (body, lang = '') => {\n  const text = esc(body);\n  let ticks = '```';\n  while (text.includes(ticks)) ticks += '`';\n  return `${ticks}${lang}\\n${text}\\n${ticks}`;\n};\n\nconst demote = (s) =>\n  esc(s).replace(/^(#{1,4}) /gm, (_m, h) => `${'#'.repeat(h.length + 2)} `);\n\nconst REMINDER = /<system-reminder>[\\s\\S]*?<\\/system-reminder>/g;\n\nconst textOf = (content) => {\n  if (typeof content === 'string') return content;\n  if (!Array.isArray(content)) return '';\n  return content\n    .filter((b) => b.type === 'text')\n    .map((b) => b.text)\n    .join('\\n\\n');\n};\n\nconst who =\n  records.find((r) => r.gitBranch !== undefined && r.userType)?.userType ===\n  'external'\n    ? 'User'\n    : 'User';\n\nconst out = [];\nlet turn = 0;\nlet firstTs = null;\nlet lastTs = null;\nconst stats = { prompts: 0, replies: 0, tools: 0, results: 0, injected: 0, errors: 0 };\n\nfor (const r of records) {\n  if (r.timestamp) {\n    if (firstTs === null) firstTs = r.timestamp;\n    lastTs = r.timestamp;\n  }\n\n  if (r.type === 'user') {\n    const c = r.message && r.message.content;\n    const results = Array.isArray(c) ? c.filter((b) => b.type === 'tool_result') : [];\n    if (results.length) {\n      for (const res of results) {\n        stats.results++;\n        if (res.is_error) stats.errors++;\n        const body =\n          typeof res.content === 'string'\n            ? res.content\n            : Array.isArray(res.content)\n              ? res.content.map((b) => (b.type === 'text' ? b.text : `[${b.type}]`)).join('\\n')\n              : JSON.stringify(res.content == null ? '' : res.content, null, 2);\n        out.push(\n          `<details><summary>result${res.is_error ? ' — error' : ''}</summary>\\n\\n${fence(body)}\\n\\n</details>\\n`\n        );\n      }\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const raw = textOf(c);\n    const reminders = raw.match(REMINDER) || [];\n    const spoken = raw.replace(REMINDER, '').trim();\n\n    if (!spoken) {\n      if (reminders.length) {\n        stats.injected++;\n        out.push(\n          `<details><summary>injected context</summary>\\n\\n${fence(reminders.join('\\n\\n'))}\\n\\n</details>\\n`\n        );\n      }\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    stats.prompts++;\n    turn++;\n    out.push(`\\n---\\n\\n## ${turn}. ${who}\\n\\n${demote(spoken)}\\n`);\n    if (reminders.length) {\n      stats.injected++;\n      out.push(\n        `<details><summary>injected context delivered with this message</summary>\\n\\n${fence(reminders.join('\\n\\n'))}\\n\\n</details>\\n`\n      );\n    }\n    continue;\n  }\n\n  if (r.type === 'assistant') {\n    const c = r.message && r.message.content;\n    if (!Array.isArray(c)) continue;\n    for (const b of c) {\n      if (b.type === 'text') {\n        const t = b.text.trim();\n        if (t) {\n          stats.replies++;\n          out.push(`${demote(t)}\\n`);\n        }\n      } else if (b.type === 'tool_use') {\n        stats.tools++;\n        out.push(`**→ ${b.name}**\\n\\n${fence(JSON.stringify(b.input, null, 2), 'json')}\\n`);\n      }\n      // `thinking` is skipped; see the header.\n    }\n    continue;\n  }\n\n  if (r.type === 'system' && r.content) {\n    out.push(`> _system:_ ${esc(r.content).trim().split('\\n').join('\\n> ')}\\n`);\n  }\n}\n\nconst sessionId = (records.find((r) => r.sessionId) || {}).sessionId || 'unknown';\nconst day = (lastTs || new Date().toISOString()).slice(0, 10);\nconst dest =\n  process.argv[3] || path.join(repoRoot, 'planning', `TRANSCRIPT-${day}.md`);\n\nconst header = [\n  '# Session transcript',\n  '',\n  'Generated by `bin/transcript` from the Claude Code JSONL log. Every prompt,',\n  'reply, tool call and tool result, in order and in full.',\n  '',\n  'Three things to know about what is here:',\n  '',\n  \"- **The model's reasoning is not in it.** Every `thinking` block in the log is\",\n  '  an empty string carrying only a signature, so it is not persisted and cannot',\n  '  be recovered. What is below is what was said and done, not what was weighed.',\n  '- **Tool results and injected context are collapsed.** Expand them for the',\n  '  detail; the conversation reads straight through without. Context the harness',\n  '  injected is labelled as such rather than counted as something anybody said.',\n  '- **Only headings were altered.** Heading levels inside replies are demoted by',\n  '  two so they do not collide with the turn headings. Nothing else is changed.',\n  '',\n  '| | |',\n  '| --- | --- |',\n  `| Session | \\`${sessionId}\\` |`,\n  `| Began | ${firstTs || 'unknown'} |`,\n  `| Ended | ${lastTs || 'unknown'} |`,\n  `| Prompts | ${stats.prompts} |`,\n  `| Replies | ${stats.replies} |`,\n  `| Tool calls | ${stats.tools} |`,\n  `| Tool results | ${stats.results}, of which ${stats.errors} errors |`,\n  `| Injected-context blocks | ${stats.injected} |`,\n  '',\n].join('\\n');\n\nfs.writeFileSync(dest, header + out.join('\\n'));\nconsole.log(\n  `${path.relative(repoRoot, dest)} — ${stats.prompts} prompts, ${stats.replies} replies, ${stats.tools} tool calls`\n);\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x bin/transcript; ls -l bin/transcript; node -c \"\" 2>/dev/null; node --check bin/transcript && echo \"syntax ok\"",
  "description": "Write bin/transcript"
}
````

Plan mode's off, and here's the problem it caught me mid-way through: **my no-argument default picked the wrong session.** It chose newest-by-mtime, which is your *other* active session (`958133df`), not this one — and overwrote the good transcript with a different conversation entirely.

Fixing the design flaw rather than just re-running: with concurrent sessions, "newest" silently renders the wrong thing.

**→ Bash**

```json
{
  "command": "python3 - <<'PY'\np='bin/transcript'\ns=open(p).read()\n\nold_start = s.index(\"function resolveSource(arg) {\")\nold_end = s.index(\"const src = resolveSource(process.argv[2]);\")\nnew_fn = '''function sessions() {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(logDir)) return [];\n  return fs\n    .readdirSync(logDir)\n    .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.jsonl'))\n    .map((f) => {\n      const full = path.join(logDir, f);\n      return { id: f.replace(/\\\\.jsonl$/, ''), path: full, at: fs.statSync(full).mtime };\n    })\n    .sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);\n}\n\n/** The first thing a person actually typed, to tell one session from another. */\nfunction opener(file) {\n  try {\n    for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\\\\n')) {\n      let r;\n      try {\n        r = JSON.parse(line);\n      } catch {\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (r.type !== 'user') continue;\n      const c = r.message && r.message.content;\n      if (Array.isArray(c) && c.some((b) => b.type === 'tool_result')) continue;\n      const spoken = textOf(c).replace(REMINDER, '').trim();\n      if (spoken) return spoken.split('\\\\n')[0].slice(0, 60);\n    }\n  } catch {\n    // Unreadable is not fatal here; the row just goes without a label.\n  }\n  return '';\n}\n\nfunction resolveSource(arg) {\n  if (arg && arg !== '--latest' && fs.existsSync(arg)) return arg;\n\n  const all = sessions();\n  if (!all.length) {\n    console.error(`No .jsonl session logs for this repo in ${logDir}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  if (arg === '--latest') return all[0].path;\n\n  if (arg) {\n    const named = all.find((s) => s.id === arg || s.id.startsWith(arg));\n    if (named) return named.path;\n    console.error(`No session log matching \"${arg}\" — looked in ${logDir}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  // Deliberately no default. Sessions run concurrently — a background job and\n  // whatever is in the foreground both append — so \"the newest log\" is very\n  // often somebody else's conversation, and rendering the wrong one is a\n  // mistake nothing downstream catches. It cost exactly that once. Name the\n  // session, or ask for --latest and mean it.\n  console.error('Which session? Recent ones for this repo:\\\\n');\n  for (const s of all.slice(0, 10)) {\n    const line = opener(s.path);\n    console.error(\n      `  ${s.id}  ${s.at.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 16)}  ${line}`\n    );\n  }\n  console.error('\\\\n  bin/transcript <session-id>     that session');\n  console.error('  bin/transcript --latest         the most recently active');\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\n'''\ns = s[:old_start] + new_fn + s[old_end:]\n\n# The usage header has to describe what it now does.\ns = s.replace(\n\"\"\"//   bin/transcript                    the most recent session for this repo\n//   bin/transcript <session-id>       one named session\n//   bin/transcript <file.jsonl>       a log anywhere on disk\n//   bin/transcript <src> <dest.md>    an explicit destination\"\"\",\n\"\"\"//   bin/transcript                    list the recent sessions and stop\n//   bin/transcript <session-id>       one named session, by id or a prefix\n//   bin/transcript --latest           whichever log was written to last\n//   bin/transcript <file.jsonl>       a log anywhere on disk\n//   bin/transcript <src> <dest.md>    an explicit destination\n//\n// There is no default session on purpose: a background job and a foreground\n// one both append as they go, so the newest log is frequently not the\n// conversation you meant, and nothing downstream would catch the substitution.\"\"\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nprint('patched')\nPY\nnode --check bin/transcript && echo \"syntax ok\"",
  "description": "Make session selection explicit"
}
